# TY2025 board learning checkpoint — October 6, 2026

## Compacted status (2026-10-05)

The existing checklist has **52 open TODOs**. The [completed ledger](docs/mef/ty2025/readiness/ty2025-product-board-completed-2026-10-01.md) records **1,348 bounded slices**; these do not close their broader parent requirements. The main checklist is frozen. New future work belongs only in `future_todo` and is outside the execution queue.

The latest full regression of code at `a268f60c` passed **11,233 tests, 0 failed, 0 ignored**, with real Poppler 26.09.0 in 40m17s. The [validation batch](docs/mef/ty2025/testing/ty2025-form1040-validation-batch.md) records commands, hashes, and focused source/native/PDF/XSD evidence. Earlier pending-run statements are superseded by this result.

The selected synthetic packet contains 175 exportable cases and 1,073 filled pages; the separate MFS mortgage fixture also passes. These results do not prove every filing route, every page, source authenticity, IRS business rules, or ATS acceptance. Named-form source and positive-route gaps remain open.

Four later positive-route slices add the one-policy family Form 8962 repayment caps, MFS AMT basis crossover, larger Section A patent gifts, and a repaired full-return Form 8864 producer packet. The integrated positive-route check passes 19/19; the final Form 8864/review checks pass 4/4. A new whole-repository regression is required for this code. The planner now has 188 fixtures covering 86 of 113 PDF keys, leaving 27 uncovered keys.

The [dependency audit](docs/mef/ty2025/inventory/ty2025-nonnamed-board-dependency-audit.md) identifies work within the existing queue. Detailed accumulated learnings are preserved in the [October 5 checkpoint](docs/mef/ty2025/readiness/ty2025-product-board-status-2026-10-05.md) and [earlier checkpoint](docs/mef/ty2025/readiness/ty2025-product-board-status-2026-10-04.md).

IRS ATS was reported unavailable through October 13, 2026, 9 a.m. Eastern, with R10.A WSDL installation scheduled that day. Recheck operational status and accepted versions before transmission. Credentials, IRS acceptance, and unresolved Scenario 1/8/13 source conflicts remain release gates. Do not close parents until their full requirements are verified.

# Form 8621 parent parity scope audit (read-only, main 2b3a2e17b)

Official primary sources: https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/f8621.pdf (Dec. 2025 four-page form) and https://www.irs.gov/instructions/i8621 (12/2025 instructions). Local baseline: `docs/mef/ty2025/domains/income/foreign/form8621/ty2025-form8621-pdf-gap.md`; typed calculation `forms/f1040/nodes/inputs/income/foreign/f8621/index.ts` and `excess_distribution.ts`; staged parent `forms/f1040/2025/domains/income/foreign/form8621/form8621_parent_source.ts`, `pdf/forms/f8621_parent_source.ts`, `pdf/forms/f8621_packet_source.ts`; native `mef/forms/f8621.ts`. Form 8621 native is registered; parent PDF is absent from `pdf/forms/index.ts`.

## Current contract versus complete parent

- Shared header/Part I: `parent_source` has corporation address/year, share classes, joint ownership, acquisition date, enum election status, issuer `document_id` and declared SHA-256. The projector sums shares/value and aligns status with regime. It does **not** retain or hash the issuer's actual bytes, bind reviewed account/transaction/ownership records to first PFIC year, or establish annual filing/Part I exception status. Shareholder/filer facts come from final identity. One parent PDF per PFIC plus true continuation/statement pages must be registered and visually checked, with native/PDF and actual full-1040 joins.
- Section 1291 Part V: calculator has dated USD/foreign distribution/disposition events, prior 2022–24 distribution amounts, block shares, first PFIC year, spot references, per-year foreign-tax-credit scalars and calculated tax/interest. `parent_source` adds locators/digests for prior distribution history but no retained record bytes. Staged PDF handles only 1291, source checks current Schedule 1 8z, Form1040 Form8621 tax and Schedule2 17p; one Part V per positive event and statement. Complete source proof needs account/broker distribution and disposition records (including zero history records where line15b uses them), block acquisition/basis, PFIC status/first year, actual spot quote records, and any creditable foreign tax/withholding; recompute all lines 15/16 and native/PDF/1040 joins. Prior accepted returns are not intrinsically required to establish ordinary prior distribution amounts or default 1291 status; they become necessary only for claims derived from a prior filed election/tax treatment.
- QEF Part III: item has ordinary income, capital gain and optional §951/§1293(g) reductions; native prints 6a–7c but there is no QEF parent PDF route. Needed: retained PFIC Annual Information Statement (or permitted intermediary/combined statement) with verified actual bytes and parsed pro-rata earnings/gain categories, ownership fraction and tax year; actual reduction sources; new-vs-continuing QEF election and acquisition/first PFIC year. QEF distributions require 8a–8e and prior inclusion/basis ledger; an asserted nontaxable prior inclusion requires historical filed-return evidence, not merely a scalar. Current 1294 Election B needs separate 9a/9b/9c tax-with/without calculation and Form1040 line24 reduction. A new 2025 QEF election based on current-year records does not automatically need a prior accepted return; continuing election or prior inclusion claims do.
- Section 1296 MTM Part IV: item has year-end FMV, adjusted basis and unreversed inclusions; graph computes limited gain/loss and native prints only year-end fields. Needed: actual exchange/marketability evidence; dated share lots, transaction and basis-adjustment ledger; historical MTM inclusion/deduction ledger for unreversed inclusions; lines 13–14c and separate statements for dispositions; new-vs-continuing election. A new election after the first held year may trigger a section 1291 deemed-sale/distribution calculation under official coordination rules. Continuing status depends on prior effective election evidence; a first-year 2025 election does not inherently need a prior accepted return.
- Section 1294 election/Part VI: current `no_outstanding_section1294_election: true` deliberately excludes all outstanding/terminated cases. For **new 2025 Election B**, source must establish QEF undistributed earnings, no disqualifying §951 amount or disposed shares, current election timing, Part III lines 8a–9c, and the actual Form1040 line24 reduction. For **outstanding prior elections**, require each prior effective election/accepted filed-return record, prior Form8621 line 9c or line25 carryover, undisbursed earnings, accrued section6621 interest to filing date, and all termination events (distribution, deemed distribution/loan/pledge/guarantee, stock disposition, change of status). Calculate Part VI lines 17–26 per election, including partial termination and Schedule2 lines17z/17q. The prior accepted-return gate applies here because carried deferred tax is a prior filed election claim. A blanket lack of prior-accepted proof should not block an unrelated current first-year 1291/QEF/MTM route.
- Part II elections D–H, qualifying-insurance and atypical indirect-owner/first-chain cases are additional conditional parent fields; they need their specified deemed-sale/dividend, status, E&P/basis and attachment facts before those boxes can be filed. The ordinary three regimes cannot silently imply or suppress these elections.

## Source/evidence rule to implement

Keep ordinary reviewed source facts for records that do not require a filed/retained document; where the IRS requires the shareholder to retain a copy (notably annual QEF/intermediary statements), verify the bytes, SHA-256 and parsed facts of that retained copy. A source `document_id` plus caller-supplied 64-character digest is only a locator. Require prior filing/acceptance evidence only for a claim whose validity or carryover depends on an earlier filed election or recognized inclusion/deferred tax. Do not turn absence of external authentication into a blanket 8621 prohibition. Still distinguish local XSD/filled-PDF proof from IRS business-rule/ATS acceptance.

## Implementation/proof sequence

1. Add typed share-lot, statement, trading/basis, distribution/disposition and historical election/deferral evidence by route; reject cross-regime/scalar conflicts. Retain verified copies and link every fact to its actual source where required. Establish annual filing and applicable Part I facts.
2.
